{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_012cgJEzgBqCapiz4ZExcSYg","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01VZ3MFcztJkrse9Pv43RvSN","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"westd-27271","command":"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && mkdir -p /root/autodl-tmp/importance_margin_vla_compress/figures && cat > /tmp/fig_method_gap.py << 'PYEOF'\nimport matplotlib\nmatplotlib.use('Agg')\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nimport matplotlib.patches as mpatches\nimport numpy as np\nimport os\n\nplt.rcParams.update({\n    'font.family': 'DejaVu Sans',\n    'font.size': 10,\n    'axes.titlesize': 12,\n    'axes.labelsize': 11,\n    'xtick.labelsize': 9.5,\n    'ytick.labelsize': 9.5,\n    'legend.fontsize': 9,\n    'figure.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.bbox': 'tight',\n    'savefig.pad_inches': 0.08,\n    'axes.spines.top': False,\n    'axes.spines.right': False,\n})\n\n# ── Data ──\nsuites = ['Object', 'Spatial', 'Goal', 'Long-Horizon']\nmodel_order = [r'$\\pi$0.5', 'X-VLA', 'OpenVLA', 'SmolVLA']\ncv_map = {r'$\\pi$0.5': 0.055, 'X-VLA': 0.151, 'OpenVLA': 0.215, 'SmolVLA': 0.314}\n\n# |SR(L2-norm) - SR(Random)| in pp at k=0.95\n# OpenVLA Spatial/Goal/Long at k=0.9 (k=0.95 not available)\n# X-VLA Object: no L2-norm data (None)\n# SmolVLA: both methods collapsed to ~0% SR\ngap_data = {\n    r'$\\pi$0.5':  [24.2, 21.0,  8.2, 23.8],\n    'X-VLA':      [None,  1.4,  0.2,  1.2],\n    'OpenVLA':    [ 1.4,  4.8,  4.8,  3.2],\n    'SmolVLA':    [ 0.0,  0.0,  0.0,  0.0],\n}\n\n# Colors (Okabe-Ito)\ncolors = {\n    r'$\\pi$0.5':  '#D55E00',   # vermillion\n    'X-VLA':      '#0072B2',   # blue\n    'OpenVLA':    '#56B4E9',   # sky blue\n    'SmolVLA':    '#BBBBBB',   # gray\n}\n\n# ── Layout ──\nn_suites = len(suites)\nn_models = len(model_order)\nbar_w = 0.18\ngap_between = 0.03\nx_base = np.arange(n_suites) * 1.15\n\nfig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(7, 5.25))\n\nfor mi, model in enumerate(model_order):\n    offset = (mi - (n_models - 1) / 2) * (bar_w + gap_between)\n    vals = gap_data[model]\n\n    for si in range(n_suites):\n        pos = x_base[si] + offset\n        val = vals[si]\n\n        if val is None:\n            ax.text(pos, 0.6, '—', ha='center', va='bottom', fontsize=9,\n                    color='#AAAAAA', fontweight='bold')\n            continue\n\n        bar_kw = dict(width=bar_w, color=colors[model], zorder=2,\n                      edgecolor='white', linewidth=0.6)\n\n        if model == 'SmolVLA':\n            bar_kw.update(alpha=0.30, hatch='///', edgecolor='#999999',\n                          linewidth=0.5)\n            display_h = 0.4\n        else:\n            bar_kw['alpha'] = 0.88\n            display_h = val\n\n        ax.bar(pos, display_h, **bar_kw)\n\n        # Value annotation\n        if val == 0:\n            ax.text(pos, display_h + 0.4, '0', ha='center', va='bottom',\n                    fontsize=7, color='#999999', fontstyle='italic')\n        else:\n            txt_color = colors[model]\n            ax.text(pos, val + 0.4, f'{val:.1f}', ha='center', va='bottom',\n                    fontsize=7.5, fontweight='bold', color=txt_color)\n\n# ── Reference line at 2pp ──\nax.axhline(y=2, color='#AAAAAA', linestyle='--', linewidth=0.7, zorder=1)\nax.text(x_base[-1] + 0.52, 2.4, '2 pp', fontsize=7.5, color='#AAAAAA',\n        ha='right', va='bottom', fontstyle='italic')\n\n# ── Annotations ──\n# SmolVLA footnote\nax.text(0.99, 0.015, r'$\\dagger$ SmolVLA: both methods collapsed (SR $\\approx$ 0%)',\n        transform=ax.transAxes, fontsize=7.5, color='#999999',\n        ha='right', va='bottom', fontstyle='italic')\n\n# OpenVLA k note\nax.text(0.01, 0.015, '* OpenVLA Spatial / Goal / Long at k = 0.9',\n        transform=ax.transAxes, fontsize=7.5, color='#999999',\n        ha='left', va='bottom', fontstyle='italic')\n\n# ── Axes ──\nax.set_xticks(x_base)\nax.set_xticklabels(suites)\nax.set_ylabel(r'Method Gap (pp)   $|$SR$_{\\mathrm{L2}}$ $-$ SR$_{\\mathrm{Rand}}$$|$',\n              fontsize=10.5)\nax.set_ylim(0, 29)\nax.set_xlim(x_base[0] - 0.55, x_base[-1] + 0.58)\n\n# Title\nax.set_title('Low CV amplifies pruning-method sensitivity',\n             fontsize=11.5, fontweight='medium', pad=12)\n\n# ── Legend ──\nhandles = []\nfor model in model_order:\n    kw = dict(facecolor=colors[model], edgecolor='white', linewidth=0.5)\n    if model == 'SmolVLA':\n        kw.update(alpha=0.30, hatch='///', edgecolor='#999999')\n    else:\n        kw.update(alpha=0.88)\n    label = f'{model}  (CV = {cv_map[model]:.3f})'\n    handles.append(mpatches.Patch(**kw, label=label))\n\nax.legend(handles=handles, loc='upper right', framealpha=0.95,\n          edgecolor='#DDDDDD', fancybox=False, handlelength=1.4)\n\n# Grid\nax.yaxis.grid(True, alpha=0.12, zorder=0)\nax.set_axisbelow(True)\n\nplt.tight_layout()\n\noutdir = '/root/autodl-tmp/importance_margin_vla_compress/figures'\nplt.savefig(f'{outdir}/fig_method_gap.pdf', dpi=300)\nplt.savefig(f'{outdir}/fig_method_gap.png', dpi=150)\nplt.close()\nprint(f'Saved: {outdir}/fig_method_gap.pdf and .png')\nPYEOF\npython3 /tmp/fig_method_gap.py","account":"root"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":251,"cache_read_input_tokens":77405,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":251},"output_tokens":13,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1fe6de08-e6fe-4df6-8c60-e151db3385ee","uuid":"4fac70a7-9bea-4374-b801-54b323652538"}

r'$\\pi$0.5':  '#D55E00',   # vermillion\n    'X-VLA':      '#0072B2',   # blue\n    'OpenVLA':    '#56B4E9',   # sky blue\n    'SmolVLA':    '#BBBBBB',   # gray\n}\n\n# ── Layout ──\nn_suites = len(suites)\nn_models = len(model_order)\nbar_w = 0.18\ngap_between = 0.03\nx_base = np.arange(n_suites) * 1.15\n\nfig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(7, 5.25))\n\nfor mi, model in vals[si]\n\n        if val is None:\n            ax.text(pos, 0.6, '—', ha='center', va='bottom', fontsize=9,\n                    color='#AAAAAA', fontweight='bold')\n            continue\n\n        bar_kw = dict(width=bar_w, color=colors[model], zorder=2,\n                      edgecolor='white', linewidth=0.6)\n\n        if model == 'SmolVLA':\n            bar_kw.update(alpha=0.30, hatch='///',
